function switchTab(id,btn){
  activeTab=id;
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('tab-'+id).classList.add('active');
  btn.classList.add('active');
  if(id==='timeline') renderSwimlane();
}

function isPriority(imp,eff){return imp==='H'||imp==='M';}
function capColor(pct){return pct>100?'var(--red)':pct>80?'var(--amber)':'var(--green)';}
function capColorRaw(pct){return pct>100?'#C43333':pct>80?'#C77B10':'#1A8C60';}
function cellBg(imp,eff){
  if(isPriority(imp,eff))return 'rgba(27,95,170,0.06)';
  const alphas={HL:.08,HM:.05,HH:.03,ML:.06,MM:.04,MH:.02,LL:.03,LM:.02,LH:.01};
  const a=alphas[imp+eff]||.03;
  const raw={H:'#1B5FAA',M:'#1A8C60',L:'#7A776E'}[imp];
  const hex=Math.round(a*255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0');
  return raw+hex;
}

function showToast(msg){const t=document.getElementById('move-toast');t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2400);}

function highlightRequester(reqName){
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const blocks=document.querySelectorAll('.req-block');
    blocks.forEach(b=>{
      if(b.dataset.req===reqName){
        b.classList.remove('highlight-update');
        void b.offsetWidth;
        b.classList.add('highlight-update');
        if(chevOpen[reqName]===false){chevOpen[reqName]=true;renderChevrons();}
      }
    });
  },80);
}

function zoom(dir){zoomIdx=Math.max(0,Math.min(ZOOM_STEPS.length-1,zoomIdx+dir));zoomLevel=ZOOM_STEPS[zoomIdx];applyZoom();}
function resetZoom(){zoomIdx=2;zoomLevel=1;applyZoom();}
function applyZoom(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('heatmap-wrap');
  const scroller=document.getElementById('heatmap-scroller');
  wrap.style.transform=`scale(${zoomLevel})`;
  wrap.style.transformOrigin='top left';
  wrap.style.width=(100/zoomLevel)+'%';
  setTimeout(()=>{scroller.style.height=(wrap.offsetHeight*zoomLevel+20)+'px';},30);
  document.getElementById('zoom-label').textContent=Math.round(zoomLevel*100)+'%';
}

let saveTimer=null;
function markSaving(){document.getElementById('save-dot').className='save-dot saving';document.getElementById('save-label').textContent='Saving…';}
function markSaved(){document.getElementById('save-dot').className='save-dot';document.getElementById('save-label').textContent='Saved';}
function persist(){
  markSaving();
  try{
    localStorage.setItem('q3v2-items',JSON.stringify(items));
    localStorage.setItem('q3v2-settings',JSON.stringify({
      capH:document.getElementById('cap-hours').value,
      devs:document.getElementById('devs').value,
      weeks:document.getElementById('weeks').value,
      zoomIdx
    }));
    markSaved();
  }catch(e){document.getElementById('save-label').textContent='Save failed';}
}
function saveAndRender(){clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=setTimeout(persist,400);render();}

function loadData(){
  try{const r=localStorage.getItem('q3v2-items');if(r)items=JSON.parse(r);}catch(e){}
  try{
    const r=localStorage.getItem('q3v2-settings');
    if(r){const s=JSON.parse(r);
      if(s.capH)document.getElementById('cap-hours').value=s.capH;
      if(s.devs)document.getElementById('devs').value=s.devs;
      if(s.weeks)document.getElementById('weeks').value=s.weeks;
      if(s.zoomIdx!=null){zoomIdx=s.zoomIdx;zoomLevel=ZOOM_STEPS[zoomIdx];}
    }
  }catch(e){}
  render();
  setTimeout(applyZoom,100);
}

function clearAll(){
  if(!confirm('Clear all Q3 data? This cannot be undone.'))return;
  items=[];
  localStorage.removeItem('q3v2-items');
  localStorage.removeItem('q3v2-settings');
  document.getElementById('cap-hours').value='480';
  document.getElementById('devs').value='3';
  document.getElementById('weeks').value='12';
  zoomIdx=2;zoomLevel=1;
  render();applyZoom();markSaved();
}

function syncBlocked(){
  if(parseInt(document.getElementById('f-hrs').value)===0)
    document.getElementById('f-status').value='blocked';
}
function syncStatus(){
  if(document.getElementById('f-status').value==='blocked')
    document.getElementById('f-hrs').value='0';
}
function showErr(msg){document.getElementById('err-msg').textContent=msg;if(msg.toLowerCase().includes('name')||msg.toLowerCase().includes('already'))document.getElementById('f-name').classList.add('err');}
function clearErr(){document.getElementById('err-msg').textContent='';document.getElementById('f-name').classList.remove('err');}

function validate(){
  const name=document.getElementById('f-name').value.trim();
  const req=document.getElementById('f-req').value.trim();
  const hrs=document.getElementById('f-hrs').value;
  const status=document.getElementById('f-status').value;
  if(!name){showErr('Initiative name is required.');document.getElementById('f-name').focus();return false;}
  if(name.length<2){showErr('Name must be at least 2 characters.');return false;}
  if(items.find(i=>i.name.toLowerCase()===name.toLowerCase())){showErr(`"${name}" already exists.`);return false;}
  if(!req){showErr('Requester is required.');document.getElementById('f-req').focus();return false;}
  if(hrs===''||isNaN(parseInt(hrs))||parseInt(hrs)<0){showErr('Hours must be 0 or more.');return false;}
  if(parseInt(hrs)>2000){showErr('Hours cannot exceed 2000.');return false;}
  if(status!=='blocked'&&status!=='closed'&&parseInt(hrs)===0){showErr('0 hours is only valid for Blocked or Closed.');return false;}
  return true;
}

function updateLimitBadge(){
  const badge=document.getElementById('limit-badge');
  badge.textContent=items.length+' initiative'+(items.length!==1?'s':'');
  badge.style.cssText='background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text2)';
  document.getElementById('add-btn').disabled=false;
}

function updateFilterDropdown(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('filter-req');
  const cur=sel.value;
  const reqs=[...new Set(items.map(i=>i.req))].sort();
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">All requesters</option>'+reqs.map(r=>`<option value="${r}"${r===cur?' selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('');
}

function etaStatus(eta){
  if(!eta)return null;
  const today=new Date();today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const d=new Date(eta+'T00:00:00');
  const diff=Math.round((d-today)/86400000);
  if(diff<0)return{label:`${Math.abs(diff)}d overdue`,color:'var(--red)',bg:'var(--red-bg)'};
  if(diff===0)return{label:'Due today',color:'var(--red)',bg:'var(--red-bg)'};
  if(diff<=7)return{label:`${diff}d left`,color:'var(--amber)',bg:'var(--amber-bg)'};
  if(diff<=30)return{label:`${diff}d left`,color:'var(--text2)',bg:'var(--surface2)'};
  return{label:new Date(eta+'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US',{month:'short',day:'numeric'}),color:'var(--text2)',bg:'var(--surface2)'};
}
function fmtEta(eta){
  if(!eta)return'';
  return new Date(eta+'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US',{month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'2-digit'});
}

function renderHeatmap(){
  const grid=document.getElementById('heatmap-grid');
  const yEl=document.getElementById('y-labels');
  const filterReq=document.getElementById('filter-req').value;
  const cellH=zoomLevel<0.8?80:zoomLevel>1.4?150:120;
  const sz=zoomLevel<=0.75?{av:12,fn:9,hr:8}:zoomLevel>=1.5?{av:18,fn:12,hr:10}:{av:14,fn:10,hr:9};

  yEl.style.justifyContent='space-around';
  yEl.innerHTML=ROWS.map(r=>`<div class="y-label" style="min-height:${cellH}px">${IMP_LABEL[r]}</div>`).join('');
  grid.innerHTML='';

  ROWS.forEach(imp=>{
    COLS.forEach(eff=>{
      const cell=document.createElement('div');
      cell.className='hm-cell'+(isPriority(imp,eff)?' priority':imp==='L'?' low-impact':'');
      cell.style.cssText=`background:${cellBg(imp,eff)};min-height:${cellH}px`;
      cell.dataset.imp=imp;cell.dataset.eff=eff;

      cell.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();cell.classList.add('drag-over');});
      cell.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>cell.classList.remove('drag-over'));
      cell.addEventListener('drop',e=>{
        e.preventDefault();cell.classList.remove('drag-over');
        const name=e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain')||dragName;
        if(!name)return;
        const it=items.find(i=>i.name===name);
        if(!it)return;
        const oldImp=it.imp,oldEff=it.eff;
        const newImp=cell.dataset.imp,newEff=cell.dataset.eff;
        if(oldImp===newImp&&oldEff===newEff)return;
        it.imp=newImp;it.eff=newEff;
        showToast(`"${it.name}" → ${IMP_LABEL[newImp]} · ${newEff==='H'?'High':newEff==='M'?'Medium':'Low'} effort`);
        highlightRequester(it.req);
        saveAndRender();
      });

      const cellItems=items.filter(i=>i.imp===imp&&i.eff===eff);
      const visItems=filterReq?cellItems.filter(i=>i.req===filterReq):cellItems;
      const dimItems=filterReq?cellItems.filter(i=>i.req!==filterReq):[];
      const container=document.createElement('div');
      container.className='cell-items';

      if(!cellItems.length){
        const hint=document.createElement('div');
        hint.className='empty-hint';
        hint.innerHTML=`<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"><path d="M8 6h13M8 12h13M8 18h13M3 6h.01M3 12h.01M3 18h.01"/></svg>Drop here`;
        container.appendChild(hint);
      }else{
        [...visItems,...dimItems]
          .sort((a,b)=>(SORT_ORDER[a.status]||9)-(SORT_ORDER[b.status]||9))
          .forEach(it=>{
            const pal=rclr(it.req);
            const isDimmed=filterReq&&it.req!==filterReq;
            const stColRaw=STATUS_COL_RAW[it.status];
            const isClosed=it.status==='closed';
            const chip=document.createElement('div');
            chip.className='ini-chip'+
              (it.status==='deferred'?' deferred':'')+
              (it.status==='inprogress'?' inprogress':'')+
              (it.status==='blocked'?' blocked':'')+
              (isClosed?' closed':'');
            chip.style.cssText=`background:${it.status==='blocked'?'var(--red-bg)':isClosed?'var(--purple-bg)':IMP_BG[it.imp]};border-color:${it.status==='blocked'?'var(--red)':it.status==='inprogress'?STATUS_COL[it.status]:stColRaw+'44'};opacity:${isDimmed?'0.15':'1'}`;
            chip.title=`${it.name} · ${it.req} · ${STATUS_LABEL[it.status]}${it.eta?' · '+fmtEta(it.eta):''}\nDrag to reprioritize`;
            chip.draggable=!isDimmed&&!isClosed;
            chip.innerHTML=`
              <div class="chip-sdot" style="background:${STATUS_COL[it.status]}"></div>
              <div class="chip-avatar" style="width:${sz.av}px;height:${sz.av}px;font-size:${sz.av-6}px;background:${pal.bg};color:${pal.fg}">${initials(it.req)}</div>
              <span class="chip-name" style="font-size:${sz.fn}px;color:${it.status==='blocked'?'var(--red)':isClosed?'var(--purple)':IMP_COLOR[it.imp]};text-decoration:${isClosed?'line-through':'none'}">${it.name}</span>
              ${isClosed?`<span class="chip-closed-arrow" style="font-size:${sz.hr-1}px">▶| ${it.eta?fmtEta(it.eta):'Done'}</span>`:
                it.eta?(()=>{const e=etaStatus(it.eta);return`<span style="font-size:${sz.hr-1}px;font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px;background:${e.bg};color:${e.color};white-space:nowrap">${e.label}</span>`;})():''}
              <span class="chip-hrs" style="font-size:${sz.hr}px;color:${it.status==='blocked'?'var(--red)':isClosed?'var(--purple)':IMP_COLOR[it.imp]}">${it.hrs===0?(isClosed?'✓':'BLK'):it.hrs+'h'}</span>
            `;
            if(!isClosed){
              chip.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{
                dragName=it.name;chip.classList.add('dragging');
                e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',it.name);
              });
              chip.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{
                chip.classList.remove('dragging');dragName=null;
                document.querySelectorAll('.hm-cell').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('drag-over'));
              });
            }
            container.appendChild(chip);
          });

        const totHrs=(filterReq?visItems:cellItems).filter(i=>i.status!=='blocked'&&i.status!=='closed').reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);
        const bCt=(filterReq?visItems:cellItems).filter(i=>i.status==='blocked').length;
        const clCt=(filterReq?visItems:cellItems).filter(i=>i.status==='closed').length;
        const footer=document.createElement('div');
        footer.className='cell-footer';
        footer.innerHTML=`<span>${bCt?`<span style="color:var(--red)">${bCt} blocked</span>`:clCt?`<span style="color:var(--purple)">${clCt} closed</span>`:'&nbsp;'}</span><span>${totHrs}h</span>`;
        container.appendChild(footer);
      }
      cell.appendChild(container);
      grid.appendChild(cell);
    });
  });
}

/* ── SWIMLANE ── */
function renderSwimlane(){
  const el=document.getElementById('swimlane-content');
  const groupBy=document.getElementById('sl-group').value;
  const showFilter=document.getElementById('sl-show').value;

  // Date range: Q3 default Jul 1 – Sep 30 current year, or use min/max eta
  const today=new Date();today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const yr=today.getFullYear();
  let rangeStart=new Date(yr,6,1); // Jul 1
  let rangeEnd=new Date(yr,8,30);  // Sep 30

  // Expand range if ETAs or start dates fall outside
  items.forEach(it=>{
    if(it.eta){
      const d=new Date(it.eta+'T00:00:00');
      if(d<rangeStart)rangeStart=new Date(d.getTime()-7*86400000);
      if(d>rangeEnd)rangeEnd=new Date(d.getTime()+7*86400000);
    }
    if(it.start){
      const d=new Date(it.start+'T00:00:00');
      if(d<rangeStart)rangeStart=new Date(d.getTime()-7*86400000);
    }
    (it.stages||[]).forEach(s=>{
      if(s.start){const d=new Date(s.start+'T00:00:00');if(d<rangeStart)rangeStart=new Date(d.getTime()-7*86400000);}
      if(s.end){const d=new Date(s.end+'T00:00:00');if(d>rangeEnd)rangeEnd=new Date(d.getTime()+7*86400000);}
    });
  });

  // Show items based on filter
  let visItems=items.filter(it=>{
    if(showFilter==='active')return it.status!=='closed'&&it.status!=='deferred';
    if(showFilter==='closed')return it.status==='closed';
    return true;
  });

  // Items without ETA can still show as a dot at today or just their status
  const rangeMs=rangeEnd-rangeStart;

  function pct(date){return Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(date-rangeStart)/rangeMs*100));}

  // Group items
  let groups={};
  if(groupBy==='status'){
    const order=['inprogress','approved','proposed','closed','blocked','deferred'];
    order.forEach(s=>{
      const its=visItems.filter(i=>i.status===s);
      if(its.length)groups[STATUS_LABEL[s]]=its;
    });
  }else if(groupBy==='impact'){
    ['H','M','L'].forEach(imp=>{
      const its=visItems.filter(i=>i.imp===imp);
      if(its.length)groups[IMP_LABEL[imp]]=its;
    });
  }else{
    const reqs=[...new Set(visItems.map(i=>i.req))].sort();
    reqs.forEach(r=>{groups[r]=visItems.filter(i=>i.req===r);});
  }

  if(!visItems.length){
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="sl-empty">No initiatives to display.<br><span style="font-size:10px">Add initiatives and set an ETA to see them here.</span></div>`;
    return;
  }

  // Build month ticks
  const months=[];
  let cur=new Date(rangeStart.getFullYear(),rangeStart.getMonth(),1);
  while(cur<=rangeEnd){months.push(new Date(cur));cur=new Date(cur.getFullYear(),cur.getMonth()+1,1);}

  const todayPct=pct(today);

  let html=`<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="sl-table">`;
  // Header row: label col + month cols
  html+=`<tr><th class="sl-lane-header first" style="width:220px;min-width:180px">Initiative</th><th style="padding:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)"><div style="position:relative;height:28px">`;
  // Today line in header
  if(todayPct>=0&&todayPct<=100)
    html+=`<div class="sl-today-line" style="left:${todayPct}%"><div class="sl-today-label">Today</div></div>`;
  // Month labels
  months.forEach(m=>{
    const mp=pct(m);
    if(mp>=0&&mp<=100)
      html+=`<div style="position:absolute;left:${mp}%;top:4px;font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--text3);white-space:nowrap;transform:translateX(-4px)">${m.toLocaleDateString('en-US',{month:'short'})}</div>`;
  });
  html+=`</div></th></tr>`;

  Object.entries(groups).forEach(([grpName,its])=>{
    const isOpen=slGroupOpen[grpName]!==false;
    const grpId='slg-'+grpName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_');
    const pal=rclr(its[0]?.req||grpName);
    const totalHrs=its.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);
    const blockedCt=its.filter(i=>i.status==='blocked').length;

    // Collapsible group header row
    html+=`<tr class="sl-group-row" style="cursor:pointer;user-select:none" onclick="toggleSlGroup('${grpName.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">
      <td colspan="2" style="padding:6px 12px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
          <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);transition:transform .2s;display:inline-block;transform:rotate(${isOpen?90:0}deg)" id="${grpId}-chev">›</span>
          <span>${grpName}</span>
          <span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;background:var(--border);color:var(--text2);padding:1px 7px;border-radius:20px">${its.length}</span>
          <span style="margin-left:auto;font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:${blockedCt?'var(--red)':'var(--text2)'}">${blockedCt?`${blockedCt} blocked · `:''}${totalHrs}h</span>
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>`;
    its.sort((a,b)=>(SORT_ORDER[a.status]||9)-(SORT_ORDER[b.status]||9)).forEach(it=>{
      const isClosed=it.status==='closed';
      const pal=rclr(it.req);
      const etaDate=it.eta?new Date(it.eta+'T00:00:00'):null;
      const startDate=it.start?new Date(it.start+'T00:00:00'):null;
      const etaPct=etaDate?pct(etaDate):null;
      const startPct=startDate?pct(startDate):null;

      // Bar: starts at startDate (or rangeStart if no start), ends at ETA
      // If no ETA, draw a "pill" at today position
      let barLeft=0,barRight=100;
      if(etaDate){
        barLeft=startDate?Math.max(startPct,0):0;
        barRight=Math.min(etaPct,100);
      }else if(startDate){
        barLeft=Math.max(startPct,0);
        barRight=Math.min(todayPct+3,100);
      }else{
        barLeft=Math.max(todayPct-3,0);
        barRight=Math.min(todayPct+3,100);
      }
      const barW=Math.max(barRight-barLeft,0.5);
      const barCol=STATUS_COL_RAW[it.status];
      const barBg=it.status==='blocked'?'#C4333322':it.status==='closed'?'#6B3FA022':
        it.status==='inprogress'?'#C77B1022':it.status==='approved'?'#1A8C6022':'#1B5FAA18';

      // Stage sub-bars
      const STAGE_COLORS={dev:'#1B5FAA',alpha:'#6B3FA0',beta:'#C77B10',rollout:'#1A8C60'};
      const stages=it.stages||[];
      let stageBarsHtml='';
      const hasStages=stages.some(s=>s.start||s.end);
      if(hasStages){
        const stageOffsets={dev:0,alpha:3,beta:6,rollout:9};
        stageBarsHtml=`<div class="sl-stages-wrap">`;
        stages.forEach(s=>{
          const sStart=s.start?new Date(s.start+'T00:00:00'):null;
          const sEnd=s.end?new Date(s.end+'T00:00:00'):null;
          if(!sStart&&!sEnd)return;
          const sLeft=sStart?Math.max(pct(sStart),0):Math.max(todayPct-1,0);
          const sRight=sEnd?Math.min(pct(sEnd),100):Math.min(todayPct+1,100);
          const sW=Math.max(sRight-sLeft,0.5);
          const sCol=STAGE_COLORS[s.id]||'#888';
          const topOffset=stageOffsets[s.id]||0;
          stageBarsHtml+=`<div class="sl-stage-bar" style="left:${sLeft}%;width:${sW}%;background:${sCol};top:${topOffset}px;height:7px" title="${s.label}: ${s.start||'?'} → ${s.end||'?'}"></div>`;
        });
        stageBarsHtml+=`</div>`;
      }

      const metaStart=startDate?` · Start ${fmtEta(it.start)}`:'';

      html+=`<tr class="sl-row sl-group-item-${grpId}"${isOpen?'':' style="display:none"'}>
        <td class="sl-label-cell">
          <div class="sl-item-name">
            <div style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${barCol};flex-shrink:0"></div>
            <span style="${isClosed?'text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--purple)':''}">${it.name}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="sl-item-meta">${it.req} · ${it.hrs===0?(isClosed?'Closed':'Blocked'):it.hrs+'h'}${metaStart}${etaDate?` · ETA ${fmtEta(it.eta)}`:' · No ETA'}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:4px">
            <span class="sl-status-pill" style="background:${barCol}18;color:${barCol}">${STATUS_LABEL[it.status]}${isClosed?' ▶|':''}</span>
            <span class="sl-status-pill" style="background:${IMP_BG[it.imp]};color:${STATUS_COL_RAW[{H:'H',M:'M',L:'L'}[it.imp]]||'var(--text2)'};margin-left:3px">${IMP_LABEL[it.imp]}</span>
            ${hasStages?`<span class="sl-status-pill" style="background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text2);margin-left:3px">4 stages</span>`:''}
          </div>
        </td>
        <td class="sl-bar-cell">
          <div class="sl-timeline-track" style="position:relative;height:${hasStages?48:34}px">
            ${months.map(m=>{const mp=pct(m);return mp>=0&&mp<=100?`<div style="position:absolute;left:${mp}%;top:0;bottom:0;width:1px;background:var(--border);opacity:.5"></div>`:''}).join('')}
            ${todayPct>=0&&todayPct<=100?`<div class="sl-today-line" style="left:${todayPct}%"></div>`:''}
            ${etaDate?`<div class="sl-bar" style="left:${barLeft}%;width:${barW}%;background:${barBg};color:${barCol};border:1px solid ${barCol}44;top:6px;height:18px;transform:none;position:absolute" title="${it.name} · ${STATUS_LABEL[it.status]}${startDate?' · Start '+fmtEta(it.start):''} · ETA ${fmtEta(it.eta)}">
              <span class="sl-bar-label" style="font-size:9px">${it.name}</span>
              ${startDate?`<span style="margin-left:4px;font-size:8px;opacity:.7">${fmtEta(it.start)}</span>`:''}
              ${isClosed?`<span style="margin-left:4px;font-size:11px">▶|</span>`:''}
            </div>
            <div style="position:absolute;left:${Math.min(etaPct,100)}%;top:6px;transform:translate(-50%,0);width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;margin-top:5px;background:${barCol};border:2px solid var(--surface);z-index:2" title="ETA: ${fmtEta(it.eta)}"></div>`
            :`<div style="position:absolute;left:${barLeft}%;top:6px;background:${barCol};opacity:.3;height:18px;width:${barW}%;border-radius:4px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center"><span style="font-size:8px;font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;color:${barCol};font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;padding:0 4px">No ETA</span></div>`}
            ${hasStages?`<div style="position:absolute;top:28px;left:0;right:0;height:20px">`+stages.filter(s=>s.start||s.end).map((s,si)=>{
              const sStart=s.start?new Date(s.start+'T00:00:00'):null;
              const sEnd=s.end?new Date(s.end+'T00:00:00'):null;
              const sLeft=sStart?Math.max(pct(sStart),0):Math.max(todayPct-1,0);
              const sRight=sEnd?Math.min(pct(sEnd),100):Math.min(todayPct+1,100);
              const sW=Math.max(sRight-sLeft,0.5);
              const sCol=STAGE_COLORS[s.id]||'#888';
              const top=si*7;
              return`<div class="sl-stage-bar" style="left:${sLeft}%;width:${sW}%;background:${sCol};top:${top}px" title="${s.label}: ${s.start||'?'} → ${s.end||'?'}"></div>`;
            }).join('')+`</div>`:''}
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>`;
    });
  });
  html+=`</table></div>`;
  el.innerHTML=html;
}

function toggleSlGroup(grpName){
  const grpId='slg-'+grpName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_');
  const isOpen=slGroupOpen[grpName]!==false;
  slGroupOpen[grpName]=!isOpen;
  const rows=document.querySelectorAll('.sl-group-item-'+grpId);
  rows.forEach(r=>r.style.display=isOpen?'none':'');
  const chev=document.getElementById(grpId+'-chev');
  if(chev)chev.style.transform=`rotate(${isOpen?0:90}deg)`;
}

let tlReqOpen=true;
const slGroupOpen={};
function toggleTlReq(){
  tlReqOpen=!tlReqOpen;
  document.getElementById('tl-req-body').style.display=tlReqOpen?'block':'none';
  const chev=document.getElementById('tl-req-chevron');
  chev.classList.toggle('open',tlReqOpen);
}

function renderTlRequesters(){
  const el=document.getElementById('tl-req-chevrons');
  const countEl=document.getElementById('tl-req-count');
  if(!el)return;
  if(!items.length){
    el.innerHTML='';
    if(countEl)countEl.textContent='0 requesters';
    return;
  }
  const groups={};
  items.forEach(it=>{if(!groups[it.req])groups[it.req]=[];groups[it.req].push(it);});
  const reqs=Object.keys(groups).sort();
  if(countEl)countEl.textContent=reqs.length+' requester'+(reqs.length!==1?'s':'');
  el.innerHTML='';

  reqs.forEach(req=>{
    const its=groups[req];
    const pal=rclr(req);
    const totalHrs=its.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);
    const bySt={};its.forEach(i=>{bySt[i.status]=(bySt[i.status]||0)+1;});
    const blockedCt=bySt.blocked||0;
    const isOpen=chevOpen[req]!==false;

    const wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='req-block';
    wrap.dataset.req=req;
    wrap.style.marginBottom='8px';

    const pills=Object.entries(bySt)
      .sort((a,b)=>(SORT_ORDER[a[0]]||9)-(SORT_ORDER[b[0]]||9))
      .map(([s,c])=>`<span class="req-pill" style="background:${STATUS_COL_RAW[s]}18;color:${STATUS_COL_RAW[s]}">${c} ${STATUS_LABEL[s]}${s==='closed'?' ▶|':''}</span>`)
      .join('');

    const header=document.createElement('div');
    header.className='req-header';
    header.innerHTML=`
      <div class="req-avatar" style="background:${pal.bg};color:${pal.fg}">${initials(req)}</div>
      <div style="flex:0 0 auto;margin-right:12px">
        <div class="req-name">${req}</div>
        <div class="req-sub">${its.length} initiative${its.length!==1?'s':''}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="req-pills">${pills}</div>
      <div class="req-total" style="color:${blockedCt?'var(--red)':'var(--text)'}">
        ${blockedCt?`<span style="font-size:9px;display:block;color:var(--red);font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;letter-spacing:.04em">${blockedCt} BLOCKED</span>`:''}${totalHrs}h
      </div>
      <div class="req-chevron${isOpen?' open':''}">›</div>
    `;
    header.onclick=()=>{chevOpen[req]=!isOpen;renderTlRequesters();};

    const body=document.createElement('div');
    body.className='req-body'+(isOpen?'':' hidden');

    body.innerHTML=`<div>${its
      .sort((a,b)=>(SORT_ORDER[a.status]||9)-(SORT_ORDER[b.status]||9))
      .map(it=>{
        const isClosed=it.status==='closed';
        const etaS=isClosed?null:etaStatus(it.eta);
        const barCol=STATUS_COL_RAW[it.status];
        return`<div class="req-item" style="${it.status==='blocked'?'background:#FBEBEB22':isClosed?'background:#F0E8FF22':''}">
          <div class="ri-dot" style="background:${STATUS_COL[it.status]}"></div>
          <span class="ri-name" style="${isClosed?'text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--purple)':''}">${it.name}</span>
          <span class="ri-meta">${IMP_LABEL[it.imp]} · ${it.eff==='H'?'High':it.eff==='M'?'Med':'Low'} effort</span>
          ${it.start?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text2);white-space:nowrap">▷ ${fmtEta(it.start)}</span>`:''}
          ${etaS?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:${etaS.bg};color:${etaS.color};white-space:nowrap">${etaS.label}</span>`:''}
          ${isClosed&&it.eta?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--purple-bg);color:var(--purple);white-space:nowrap">▶| ${fmtEta(it.eta)}</span>`:''}
          <span class="ri-status" style="color:${STATUS_COL[it.status]};background:${STATUS_COL_RAW[it.status]}15">
            <span class="ri-sdot" style="background:${STATUS_COL[it.status]}"></span>${STATUS_LABEL[it.status]}${isClosed?' ▶|':''}
          </span>
          <span class="ri-hrs" style="color:${it.status==='blocked'?'var(--red)':isClosed?'var(--purple)':'var(--text)'}">${it.hrs===0?(isClosed?'✓':'—'):it.hrs+'h'}</span>
        </div>`;
      }).join('')}</div>
      <div class="req-footer">
        <span>${its.length} initiatives${blockedCt?` · <strong style="color:var(--red)">${blockedCt} blocked</strong>`:''}</span>
        <strong>${totalHrs}h total</strong>
      </div>
    `;

    wrap.appendChild(header);
    wrap.appendChild(body);
    el.appendChild(wrap);
  });
}

function render(){
  const capH=Math.max(1,parseInt(document.getElementById('cap-hours').value)||480);
  const devs=Math.max(1,parseInt(document.getElementById('devs').value)||3);
  const weeks=Math.max(1,parseInt(document.getElementById('weeks').value)||12);
  const totalCap=capH*devs;
  const active=items.filter(i=>i.status!=='deferred'&&i.status!=='blocked'&&i.status!=='closed');
  const usedHrs=active.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);
  const pct=Math.round(usedHrs/totalCap*100);
  const col=capColor(pct);
  const colRaw=capColorRaw(pct);
  const inProg=items.filter(i=>i.status==='inprogress').length;
  const approved=items.filter(i=>i.status==='approved').length;
  const blocked=items.filter(i=>i.status==='blocked').length;
  const deferred=items.filter(i=>i.status==='deferred').length;
  const closed=items.filter(i=>i.status==='closed').length;

  document.getElementById('metrics-row').innerHTML=`
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val">${items.length}</div><div class="metric-lbl">Total</div></div>
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val" style="color:var(--amber)">${inProg}</div><div class="metric-lbl">In progress</div></div>
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val" style="color:var(--green)">${approved}</div><div class="metric-lbl">Approved</div></div>
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val" style="color:var(--red)">${blocked}</div><div class="metric-lbl">Blocked</div></div>
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val" style="color:var(--gray)">${deferred}</div><div class="metric-lbl">Deferred</div></div>
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val" style="color:var(--purple)">${closed}</div><div class="metric-lbl">Closed ▶|</div></div>
    <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-val" style="color:${col}">${pct}%</div><div class="metric-lbl">Capacity</div><div style="font-size:10px;margin-top:3px;font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;color:${col}">${usedHrs}/${totalCap}h</div></div>
  `;

  const fill=document.getElementById('cap-fill');
  fill.style.width=Math.min(pct,100)+'%';
  fill.style.background=colRaw;
  document.getElementById('cap-pct-label').style.color=colRaw;
  document.getElementById('cap-pct-label').textContent=pct+'%';
  document.getElementById('cap-detail').textContent=pct>100
    ?`${usedHrs-totalCap}h over capacity — consider deferring some initiatives`
    :`${totalCap-usedHrs}h remaining · ${devs} devs × ${capH}h · ${weeks} weeks`;

  const cnt=document.getElementById('q3-count');
  cnt.textContent=items.length+' initiative'+(items.length!==1?'s':'');

  updateLimitBadge();
  updateFilterDropdown();
  renderHeatmap();
  renderChevrons();
  if(activeTab==='timeline')renderSwimlane();
  setTimeout(applyZoom,50);
}

let editingName=null;
function editItem(name){editingName=name;renderChevrons();setTimeout(()=>{const f=document.querySelector('.edit-name');if(f){f.focus();f.select();}},30);}

function saveEdit(oldName){
  const safeId=oldName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_');
  const nameEl=document.getElementById('edit-name-'+safeId);
  const hrsEl=document.getElementById('edit-hrs-'+safeId);
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('edit-status-'+safeId);
  const startEl=document.getElementById('edit-start-'+safeId);
  const etaEl=document.getElementById('edit-eta-'+safeId);
  if(!nameEl||!hrsEl||!statusEl)return;
  const newName=nameEl.value.trim();
  const newHrs=Math.max(0,Math.min(2000,parseInt(hrsEl.value)||0));
  const newStatus=statusEl.value;
  const newStart=startEl?startEl.value:'';
  const newEta=etaEl?etaEl.value:'';
  if(!newName||newName.length<2){nameEl.focus();nameEl.style.borderColor='var(--red)';return;}
  if(newName.toLowerCase()!==oldName.toLowerCase()&&items.find(i=>i.name.toLowerCase()===newName.toLowerCase())){nameEl.focus();nameEl.style.borderColor='var(--red)';return;}
  if(newStatus!=='blocked'&&newStatus!=='closed'&&newHrs===0){hrsEl.focus();hrsEl.style.borderColor='var(--red)';return;}
  const it=items.find(i=>i.name===oldName);
  if(!it)return;
  // Read stages
  const devStartEl=document.getElementById('edit-dev-start-'+safeId);
  const devEndEl=document.getElementById('edit-dev-end-'+safeId);
  const alphaStartEl=document.getElementById('edit-alpha-start-'+safeId);
  const alphaEndEl=document.getElementById('edit-alpha-end-'+safeId);
  const betaStartEl=document.getElementById('edit-beta-start-'+safeId);
  const betaEndEl=document.getElementById('edit-beta-end-'+safeId);
  const rolStartEl=document.getElementById('edit-rollout-start-'+safeId);
  const rolEndEl=document.getElementById('edit-rollout-end-'+safeId);
  const stages=[];
  if(devStartEl&&(devStartEl.value||devEndEl.value))stages.push({id:'dev',label:'Development',start:devStartEl.value,end:devEndEl?devEndEl.value:''});
  if(alphaStartEl&&(alphaStartEl.value||alphaEndEl.value))stages.push({id:'alpha',label:'Alpha',start:alphaStartEl.value,end:alphaEndEl?alphaEndEl.value:''});
  if(betaStartEl&&(betaStartEl.value||betaEndEl.value))stages.push({id:'beta',label:'Beta',start:betaStartEl.value,end:betaEndEl?betaEndEl.value:''});
  if(rolStartEl&&(rolStartEl.value||rolEndEl.value))stages.push({id:'rollout',label:'Rollout',start:rolStartEl.value,end:rolEndEl?rolEndEl.value:''});
  it.name=newName;it.hrs=(newStatus==='blocked'||newStatus==='closed')?0:newHrs;it.status=newStatus;it.start=newStart;it.eta=newEta;it.stages=stages;
  editingName=null;saveAndRender();
}

function cancelEdit(){editingName=null;renderChevrons();}

function renderChevrons(){
  const el=document.getElementById('req-chevrons');
  if(!items.length){el.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:12px;font-family:\'DM Mono\',monospace;color:var(--text2);padding:16px 0">No initiatives yet. Add one above.</div>';return;}
  const groups={};
  items.forEach(it=>{if(!groups[it.req])groups[it.req]=[];groups[it.req].push(it);});
  const grandTotal=items.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);
  const grandBlocked=items.filter(i=>i.status==='blocked').length;
  el.innerHTML='';

  Object.entries(groups).sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).forEach(([req,its])=>{
    const pal=rclr(req);
    const totalHrs=its.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);
    const bySt={};
    its.forEach(i=>{bySt[i.status]=(bySt[i.status]||0)+1;});
    const blockedCt=bySt.blocked||0;
    const isOpen=chevOpen[req]!==false;

    const wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='req-block';
    wrap.dataset.req=req;

    const pills=Object.entries(bySt)
      .sort((a,b)=>(SORT_ORDER[a[0]]||9)-(SORT_ORDER[b[0]]||9))
      .map(([s,c])=>`<span class="req-pill" style="background:${STATUS_COL_RAW[s]}18;color:${STATUS_COL_RAW[s]}">${c} ${STATUS_LABEL[s]}${s==='closed'?' ▶|':''}</span>`)
      .join('');

    const header=document.createElement('div');
    header.className='req-header';
    header.innerHTML=`
      <div class="req-avatar" style="background:${pal.bg};color:${pal.fg}">${initials(req)}</div>
      <div style="flex:0 0 auto;margin-right:12px">
        <div class="req-name">${req}</div>
        <div class="req-sub">${its.length} initiative${its.length!==1?'s':''}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="req-pills">${pills}</div>
      <div class="req-total" style="color:${blockedCt?'var(--red)':'var(--text)'}">
        ${blockedCt?`<span style="font-size:9px;display:block;color:var(--red);font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;letter-spacing:.04em">${blockedCt} BLOCKED</span>`:''}${totalHrs}h
      </div>
      <div class="req-chevron${isOpen?' open':''}">›</div>
    `;
    header.onclick=()=>{chevOpen[req]=!isOpen;renderChevrons();};

    const body=document.createElement('div');
    body.className='req-body'+(isOpen?'':' hidden');
    const activeHrs=its.filter(i=>i.status!=='deferred'&&i.status!=='blocked'&&i.status!=='closed').reduce((s,i)=>s+i.hrs,0);

    body.innerHTML=`<div>${its
      .sort((a,b)=>(SORT_ORDER[a.status]||9)-(SORT_ORDER[b.status]||9))
      .map(it=>{
        const safeId=it.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_');
        if(editingName===it.name){
          const stg=it.stages||[];
          const devStg=stg.find(s=>s.id==='dev')||{};
          const alphaStg=stg.find(s=>s.id==='alpha')||{};
          const betaStg=stg.find(s=>s.id==='beta')||{};
          const rolStg=stg.find(s=>s.id==='rollout')||{};
          return`<div class="req-item editing">
            <input id="edit-name-${safeId}" class="edit-field edit-name" value="${it.name.replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" maxlength="28" placeholder="Initiative name"
              onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')saveEdit('${it.name.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}');if(event.key==='Escape')cancelEdit()"/>
            <input id="edit-hrs-${safeId}" class="edit-field edit-hrs" type="number" min="0" max="2000" value="${it.hrs}" placeholder="Hours"/>
            <select id="edit-status-${safeId}" class="edit-field edit-status">
              <option value="proposed" ${it.status==='proposed'?'selected':''}>Proposed</option>
              <option value="inprogress" ${it.status==='inprogress'?'selected':''}>In progress</option>
              <option value="approved" ${it.status==='approved'?'selected':''}>Approved</option>
              <option value="blocked" ${it.status==='blocked'?'selected':''}>Blocked</option>
              <option value="deferred" ${it.status==='deferred'?'selected':''}>Deferred</option>
              <option value="closed" ${it.status==='closed'?'selected':''}>Closed ▶|</option>
            </select>
            <input id="edit-start-${safeId}" class="edit-field" type="date" value="${it.start||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;width:130px" title="Start date"/>
            <input id="edit-eta-${safeId}" class="edit-field" type="date" value="${it.eta||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;width:130px" title="ETA / Close date"/>
            <div class="edit-actions">
              <button class="edit-save" onclick="saveEdit('${it.name.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">✓ Save</button>
              <button class="edit-cancel" onclick="cancelEdit()">✗</button>
            </div>
            <div class="stage-edit-group" style="width:100%">
              <div style="font-size:9px;font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;letter-spacing:.07em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:4px">Stages (optional)</div>
              <div class="stage-edit-row">
                <div class="stage-dot" style="background:#1B5FAA"></div>
                <span class="stage-edit-label">Development</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-dev-start-${safeId}" value="${devStg.start||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Dev start"/>
                <span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">→</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-dev-end-${safeId}" value="${devStg.end||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Dev end"/>
              </div>
              <div class="stage-edit-row">
                <div class="stage-dot" style="background:#6B3FA0"></div>
                <span class="stage-edit-label">Alpha</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-alpha-start-${safeId}" value="${alphaStg.start||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Alpha start"/>
                <span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">→</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-alpha-end-${safeId}" value="${alphaStg.end||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Alpha end"/>
              </div>
              <div class="stage-edit-row">
                <div class="stage-dot" style="background:#C77B10"></div>
                <span class="stage-edit-label">Beta</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-beta-start-${safeId}" value="${betaStg.start||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Beta start"/>
                <span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">→</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-beta-end-${safeId}" value="${betaStg.end||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Beta end"/>
              </div>
              <div class="stage-edit-row">
                <div class="stage-dot" style="background:#1A8C60"></div>
                <span class="stage-edit-label">Rollout</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-rollout-start-${safeId}" value="${rolStg.start||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Rollout start"/>
                <span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--text3)">→</span>
                <input type="date" id="edit-rollout-end-${safeId}" value="${rolStg.end||''}" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--surface);color:var(--text);outline:none" title="Rollout end"/>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>`;
        }
        const isClosed=it.status==='closed';
        const etaS=isClosed?null:etaStatus(it.eta);
        return`<div class="req-item" style="${it.status==='blocked'?'background:#FBEBEB22':isClosed?'background:#F0E8FF22':''}">
          <div class="ri-dot" style="background:${STATUS_COL[it.status]}"></div>
          <span class="ri-name" style="${isClosed?'text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--purple)':''}">${it.name}</span>
          <span class="ri-meta">${IMP_LABEL[it.imp]} · ${it.eff==='H'?'High':it.eff==='M'?'Med':'Low'} effort</span>
          ${etaS?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:${etaS.bg};color:${etaS.color};white-space:nowrap">${etaS.label}</span>`:''}
          ${it.start&&!isClosed?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text2);white-space:nowrap">▷ ${fmtEta(it.start)}</span>`:''}
          ${isClosed&&it.eta?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--purple-bg);color:var(--purple);white-space:nowrap">▶| ${fmtEta(it.eta)}</span>`:''}
          ${(it.stages||[]).some(s=>s.start||s.end)?`<span style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text3);white-space:nowrap">stages</span>`:''}
          <span class="ri-status" style="color:${STATUS_COL[it.status]};background:${STATUS_COL_RAW[it.status]}15">
            <span class="ri-sdot" style="background:${STATUS_COL[it.status]}"></span>${STATUS_LABEL[it.status]}${isClosed?' ▶|':''}
          </span>
          <span class="ri-hrs" style="color:${it.status==='blocked'?'var(--red)':isClosed?'var(--purple)':'var(--text)'}">${it.hrs===0?(isClosed?'✓':'—'):it.hrs+'h'}</span>
          <button class="ri-edit" title="Edit" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editItem('${it.name.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">✎</button>
          <button class="ri-del" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removeItem('${it.name.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">×</button>
        </div>`;
      }).join('')}</div>
      <div class="req-footer">
        <span>${its.length} initiatives${blockedCt?` · <strong style="color:var(--red)">${blockedCt} blocked</strong>`:''}${bySt.deferred?` · ${bySt.deferred} deferred`:''}</span>
        <strong>${activeHrs}h active / ${totalHrs}h total</strong>
      </div>
    `;

    wrap.appendChild(header);
    wrap.appendChild(body);
    el.appendChild(wrap);
  });

  if(Object.keys(groups).length>1){
    const tot=document.createElement('div');
    tot.className='grand-total';
    tot.innerHTML=`<span>Grand total</span><span>${grandBlocked?`<span style="color:#f87171;margin-right:12px">${grandBlocked} blocked</span>`:''}<strong>${grandTotal}h</strong></span>`;
    el.appendChild(tot);
  }
}

function addItem(){
  if(!validate())return;
  items.push({
    name:document.getElementById('f-name').value.trim(),
    req:document.getElementById('f-req').value.trim(),
    imp:document.getElementById('f-imp').value,
    eff:document.getElementById('f-eff').value,
    hrs:Math.max(0,Math.min(2000,parseInt(document.getElementById('f-hrs').value)||0)),
    status:document.getElementById('f-status').value,
    start:document.getElementById('f-start').value||'',
    eta:document.getElementById('f-eta').value||'',
    stages:[]
  });
  document.getElementById('f-name').value='';
  document.getElementById('f-hrs').value='40';
  document.getElementById('f-status').value='proposed';
  document.getElementById('f-start').value='';
  document.getElementById('f-eta').value='';
  clearErr();saveAndRender();
}

function removeItem(name){
  const idx=items.findIndex(i=>i.name===name);
  if(idx>-1){items.splice(idx,1);clearErr();saveAndRender();}
}
